HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000063/B005","root_000516/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_791434fa658bb3140137","text":"2. **The “how?” of remembrance exposed as a problem of delay.** **Evidence/cue:** surface *anna* belongs to `ء ن ي B005`, inquiry into whence or how, but `ء ن ي B001/B003` supplies deliberate delay and reaching due completion; `ذ ك ر B009` makes remembrance an actionable admonition. In 89:24 the human regrets not advancing anything. **Mechanism:** reminder arrives only when the event is due, after its capacity to guide prior action has expired. The packet supplies “that day,” recollection, interrogative impossibility, and regret; I infer lateness as the reason the reminder no longer functions. **Reading change:** *anna lahu al-dhikra* asks not only where reminder could come from, but what use admonition can have once postponed to completion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000063/B001","root_000063/B003","root_000063/B005","root_000516/B004","root_000516/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7b4382752c7f1b6574b4","text":"2. **The lost rights-document of remembrance.** Lexical evidence: `ذ ك ر B008` rights document/legal instrument; `ذ ك ر B004` mention on the tongue; `ء ن ي B005` whence/how inquiry; `ء ن ي B001` deliberate delay; `ء ن ي B003` reaching due completion. Structural cue: the ayah asks `أنى له الذكرى` after delayed remembering. Assigned role: `الذكرى` is like a right-document or admissible record that is no longer practically available once the term has matured. Abductive move: I infer a legal-temporal mechanism: delayed memory cannot now function as the needed record. Reading change: the ayah reads as a closed evidentiary moment, not only regretful psychology.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_80e10cbca83b2bfeaded","text":"89:24 confirmed that the defect in remembrance was timing: cognition immediately becomes regret over non-advance, too late to perform `ذ ك ر B009` as admonition. 89:28 then supplied a contrasting mode of motion, where the soul returns responsively rather than an object being forcibly brought.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000063/B005","root_000516/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c11f5304e39eed8587a3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:23:5\",\"root_000516\",[\"B008\"]],[\"89:23:7\",\"root_000063\",[\"B005\"]],[\"89:23:9\",\"root_000516\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Hatırlama, kabul süresi geçmiş ve artık kullanılamayan bir hak belgesi gibi işler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000281/B004","root_000516/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e5b22517f3cdf0368b1c","text":"1. **A compelled arrival that compels awareness.** **Evidence/cue:** `ج ي ء B004` is bringing or presenting and `ج ي ء B005` driving or compelling; `ذ ك ر B003` is bringing to mind, while `ء ن س B002` is perceiving something into awareness. **Mechanism:** the passive presentation supplies an external object that forces the human's previously absent internal recognition. The packet supplies presentation followed by remembering; the causal arrow between them is inferred. **Reading change:** the scene is a forced convergence of object and awareness, not two independent events joined only by time.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000059/B001","root_000281/B004","root_000281/B005","root_000516/B003","root_000516/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e946c3f4077594ba214d","text":"1. **Memory compelled by presented consequence.** Lexical evidence: `ج ي ء B004` bringing/presenting something; `ج ي ء B005` driving or compelling; `ذ ك ر B003` bringing to mind; `ذ ك ر B009` reminder/admonition; `ء ن س B001` human presence.
